## Adopted checked rows

Sources: research/20261011-m5-fix-rows.md; research/20261011-m5-settling-pass.md; research/20261011-m5-open-reads.md (CW/FK/TD/PL only). Unadopted last-reads not used.

>  M5-041: (e) the DeviceAudio step, 0x0057C876..0x0057C8E0
>
> | step | what the original does | citation | record | class |
> |---|---|---|---|---|
> | DA0 | The step runs only if anim+0xC (initialised) != 0. Otherwise see the U3 error. | 0x0057C86C..0x0057C872 | M5-041 | EXACT_SOURCE |
> | DA1 | `now = BaseStationTimer::GetCurrentTimeStamp()` (u32 ms, wall clock). This is once per UpdateStream call, before UpdateAmountToSend and the frame loop. | 0x0057C876..0x0057C87E | M5-041 | EXACT_SOURCE |
> | DA2 | If the DeviceAudio track (+0xC4) is not at its end and `IsTimeToPlay(start=+0x80, now)`, which is `kf.trigger + start <= now`, unsigned (0x004F8A96..0x004F8AA0): call `PlayOnDevice()` and then `MoveToNextKeyFrame()`. That is at most one keyframe per call. | 0x0057C8C0..0x0057C8E0 | M5-041 | EXACT_SOURCE |
> | DA3 | `DeviceAudioKeyFrame::PlayOnDevice` is `bx lr`: an empty body. The only effect is the iterator advance, which feeds `HasFramesLeft` (the +0xC4 track is checked at 0x00577E7A..0x00577E84). | 0x004FA744 | NEW (M5-041) | EXACT_SOURCE |
> | DA4 | DeviceAudio keyframes come only from JSON clips (0x00576BA8) or AppendTrack (0x00577F94). No shipped JSON clip has one (`scan.py`), and FlatBuffer clips never add one, so the shipped track is always empty. | xr | M5-041 | EXACT_SOURCE |
>
>
> - **N2 → M5-041 (IMPLEMENTATION_GAP):** the DeviceAudio wall-clock step in UpdateStream (0x0057C876..0x0057C8E0:

## Call accounting and tests

See calls.md for the complete enclosing-function call census and explicit support/handoff boundary. Regression expectations are from the adopted rows; M5FixRowsTests and M3_013_CD12 verify widths, state/gates and structured log keys/levels/channels/text/arguments. Existing M5AnimationTests cover the unchanged source spans. No new emulator or hardware run. Final test counts/time are recorded in jobs/status/B-M5-1.md.
